The declared falsifier turns a failure into the run's reported practitioner result rather than a gap.\",", "   \"runpod_compute_profile\": \"gpu_basic\",", " ", "-  \"domain_practice\": \"WHAT I READ FOR THIS STEP. (a) The aii-handbook-auto-mechanistic-interpretability field handbook (loaded in full). (b) Arditi et al., 'Refusal in Language Models Is Mediated by a Single Direction', NeurIPS 2024 / arXiv 2406.11717 — difference-in-means direction, directional ablation at every component that writes the residual stream, and the weight-orthogonalisation form that is mathematically equivalent to inference-time ablation; this is the operator family the Heretic tool implements. (c) Souly et al., 'A StrongREJECT for Empty Jailbreaks', arXiv 2402.10260 — existing evaluators systematically OVERSTATE how much refusal was removed relative to human judgement, because suppressed models produce low-specificity, caveat-laden answers; the fix is a rubric that first asks refused/not and then scores the non-refusals. (d) Wang et al., 'Refusal Direction is Universal Across Safety-Aligned Languages', arXiv 2505.17306 — an English-derived direction transfers across 14 languages 'with near-perfect effectiveness'; PolyRefuse is the reference multilingual harmful/benign set. This is the single most important prior: it is the null our anchor result contradicts, so the plan must show its own failure is a property of a specific under-dosed weight edit and not a contradiction of 2505.17306 in general. (e) 'Read-Best Is Not Steer-Best', arXiv 2609.22135 — probe-best layer is NOT steer-best; probe accuracy is a mistaken heuristic for choosing an intervention site, while steering-effective layers sit in a narrow mid-to-late normalised-depth band; paired random-direction controls give a ~26x causal gap. (f) 'Predicting Where Steering Vectors Succeed', arXiv 2604.15557 — the closest genuine neighbour to this artifact: a training-free per-layer profile (Linear Accessibility Profile, A_lin, via the unembedding applied to intermediate hidden states) predicting steering effectiveness at Spearman +0.86 to +0.91 and layer choice at +0.63 to +0.92 across 5 models and 24 concept families, explicitly beating the 'middle layer' heuristic. Reported metric convention there is Spearman rho per model, not R^2.\\n\\nBASELINES EVERY COMPARABLE PAPER REPORTS. (1) A no-op/unedited arm. (2) A norm- or energy-matched RANDOM direction arm at the same sites — the handbook makes this mandatory ('steering was indistinguishable from random perturbation' is the field's canonical negative), and 2609.22135 reports it as the headline denominator. (3) A matched principal-component / harmless-PC arm, i.e. 'any perturbation of this magnitude in a plausible subspace'. (4) Difference-in-means as the direction extractor — the handbook's standing verdict is that diff-in-means beats fancier decompositions for both detection and steering, so anything more elaborate must be raced against it. (5) For an INSTRUMENT paper specifically, the trivial predictor: 2604.15557 races against the middle-layer heuristic; this run's own art_kfCCWf7o8eJ9 established two cheap predictors (single-site causal transfer rate rho +0.732, unedited baseline refusal rate rho +0.661) that beat both its depth index and EN/L cosine with paired item-bootstrap CIs excluding zero. THE BASELINE A REVIEWER NAMES FIRST IF MISSING is the cheap one-forward-pass predictor, because the field has repeatedly found cheap causal probes beating geometric predictors. Fair tuning: every competing predictor gets its hyperparameters chosen on the SAME development split as O and is scored on the SAME held-out cells, with the same bootstrap.\\n\\nCASES AND DATA. Standard here: AdvBench / mlabonne harmful_behaviors (construction only — saturated, heavily leaked into every abliteration tool's default data, and unusable as evaluation), JailbreakBench (JBB) behaviours and their benign twins (development-grade), StrongREJECT (2402.10260) for held-out harmful items with category structure, XSTest-safe for over-refusal, PolyRefuse / RefusEU (NASK-PIB) for multilingual refusal evaluation, FLORES-200 for language-quality regression, and the six-task EN harness (ARC-C, BoolQ, HellaSwag, OBQA, PIQA, Winogrande) with Slovenian LLM Eval for utility. Known-bad: substring/keyword refusal counters (2402.10260 and this run's own art_0XmNBGkzsJc_ measured kappa +0.196 with 25.4% false 'refusals' on edited checkpoints); AdvBench as an evaluation set; and treating same-ID RefusEU rows as exact translations — this run's dataset artifact graded EN-SL correspondence as 0 true / 158 partial / 1092 conflicting / 150 none, which is why the S5X cross-translated 100-pair set exists and is the ONLY basis for paired cross-language claims.\\n\\nCONTROLS AND WHAT IS HELD CONSTANT. For a placement claim the comparison is meaningless unless TOTAL REMOVAL ENERGY and LAYER COUNT are matched between the contrasted conditions — this run already learned (art_ex4hbgThhJaL) that energy matching alone is not a control because energy-matched random edits can be destructive, so controls must be matched on energy AND on collateral (per-token NLL / KL change). Also held fixed: quantisation (NF4 throughout, since absolute levels are NF4-specific), decoding (greedy, fixed max new tokens), chat template and system-prompt policy byte-identical across languages, judge and rubric frozen and blind to condition identity, and one model resident at a time with weight hashes recorded. The confound this design is most likely to be caught on: DOSE masquerading as PLACEMENT. art_0XmNBGkzsJc_'s falsifier P7 fired exactly this way — at equal harmless KL a plain dose ladder reached a zero cross-language gap, so a 'better-placed' edit that is merely stronger proves nothing. Every placement contrast must therefore be reported at matched energy AND with a dose ladder of the losing arm as the competing explanation.\\n\\nHOW MUCH IS ENOUGH. Item counts: 60-120 harmful items per language per condition is the working range in this literature (StrongREJECT's own held-out evaluations, JBB's 100 behaviours, RefusEU's per-language eval blocks); below ~50 items a per-cell refusal rate has a binomial half-width of ~0.14 and nobody reads a 0.10 difference off it. Conditions: an instrument claim needs >= 20 held-out cells to support a Spearman with a usable CI (a rank correlation on 10 points has a 95% CI roughly [-0.1, 0.9] and is uninformative); 2604.15557 uses 24 concept families x 5 models. Models: n >= 2 families for any generalisation sentence, with the honest statement that 2 checkpoints are 2 units. Seeds/repeats: the handbook's hardest execution rule is that a single-draw causal score is a volatile random variable, so the per-layer causal profile must be estimated with an uncertainty band (item bootstrap plus a split-half stability check), not as a point curve. Reported alongside every point estimate: 95% CIs from a bootstrap that resamples the RIGHT unit (semantic items, with translations clustered), Holm correction across the pre-registered family, a permutation/placebo null that must collapse, and a minimum detectable effect so that a null is readable as powered rather than empty. An underpowered panel is fixed with more graded conditions, not more readouts.\\n\\nMEASURES AND REPORTING CONVENTIONS. The outcome is a JUDGED 4-way class on generations (REFUSED / PARTIAL / COMPLIED / INVALID), never a substring rule; PARTIAL always its own column; INVALID never folded into either, because a model that has become incoherent must not read as successful suppression. Both a STRICT (refused only) and a BROAD (refused + partial) rate are reported, because this run's own audit (art_Z3I1K3VnFZuz) showed the cross-language gap ranges +0.22..+0.71 strict versus -0.04..+0.37 broad. Judge agreement is reported WITHIN edited arms, not pooled (pooled kappa 0.913 vs 0.779 within-edited in the same data), and a judge below its gate means Rogan-Gladen-corrected rates beside raw ones plus a JUDGE_SENSITIVE flag. Collateral is reported as FLORES per-token NLL change, harmless KL, language consistency (GlotLID), truncation/validity rate and a utility macro — the handbook's rule that a correction claim must also measure disruption of already-correct cases. Attack-success under an official guard pipeline is reported SEPARATELY from refusal. Instrument quality is reported as per-language Spearman plus incremental R^2 over a nested predictor stack, each with a cell bootstrap, and — the part the field now demands — a paired difference against the cheap baseline with a CI on the DIFFERENCE, not two separate correlations placed side by side.\",", "+  \"domain_practice\": \"WHAT I READ FOR THIS STEP. (a) The aii-handbook-auto-mechanistic-interpretability field handbook (loaded in full). (b) Arditi et al., 'Refusal in Language Models Is Mediated by a Single Direction', NeurIPS 2024 / arXiv 2406.11717 - difference-in-means direction, directional ablation at every component that writes the residual stream, and the weight-orthogonalisation form that is mathematically equivalent to inference-time ablation; this is the operator family the Heretic tool implements. (c) Souly et al., 'A StrongREJECT for Empty Jailbreaks', arXiv 2402.10260 - existing evaluators systematically OVERSTATE how much refusal was removed relative to human judgement, because suppressed models produce low-specificity, caveat-laden answers; the fix is a rubric that first asks refused/not and then scores the non-refusals. (d) Wang et al., 'Refusal Direction is Universal Across Safety-Aligned Languages', arXiv 2505.17306 - an English-derived direction transfers across 14 languages 'with near-perfect effectiveness'; PolyRefuse is the reference multilingual harmful/benign set. This is the single most important prior: it is the null our anchor result contradicts, so the plan must show its own failure is a property of a specific under-dosed weight edit and not a contradiction of 2505.17306 in general. (e) 'Read-Best Is Not Steer-Best', arXiv 2609.22135 - probe-best layer is NOT steer-best; probe accuracy is a mistaken heuristic for choosing an intervention site, while steering-effective layers sit in a narrow mid-to-late normalised-depth band; paired random-direction controls give a ~26x causal gap. (f) 'Predicting Where Steering Vectors Succeed', arXiv 2604.15557 - the closest genuine neighbour to this artifact: a training-free per-layer profile (Linear Accessibility Profile, A_lin, via the unembedding applied to intermediate hidden states) predicting steering effectiveness at Spearman +0.86 to +0.91 and layer choice at +0.63 to +0.92 across 5 models and 24 concept families, explicitly beating the 'middle layer' heuristic. Reported metric convention there is Spearman rho per model, not R2.\\n\\nBASELINES EVERY COMPARABLE PAPER REPORTS. (1) A no-op/unedited arm. (2) A norm- or energy-matched RANDOM direction arm at the same sites - the handbook makes this mandatory ('steering was indistinguishable from random perturbation' is the field's canonical negative), and 2609.22135 reports it as the headline denominator. (3) A matched principal-component / harmless-PC arm, i.e. 'any perturbation of this magnitude in a plausible subspace'. (4) Difference-in-means as the direction extractor - the handbook's standing verdict is that diff-in-means beats fancier decompositions for both detection and steering, so anything more elaborate must be raced against it. (5) For an INSTRUMENT paper specifically, the trivial predictor: 2604.15557 races against the middle-layer heuristic; this run's own art_kfCCWf7o8eJ9 established two cheap predictors (single-site causal transfer rate rho +0.732, unedited baseline refusal rate rho +0.661) that beat both its depth index and EN/L cosine with paired item-bootstrap CIs excluding zero. THE BASELINE A REVIEWER NAMES FIRST IF MISSING is the cheap one-forward-pass predictor, because the field has repeatedly found cheap causal probes beating geometric predictors. Fair tuning: every competing predictor gets its hyperparameters chosen on the SAME development split as O and is scored on the SAME held-out cells, with the same bootstrap.\\n\\nCASES AND DATA. Standard here: AdvBench / mlabonne harmful_behaviors (construction only - saturated, heavily leaked into every abliteration tool's default data, and unusable as evaluation), JailbreakBench (JBB) behaviours and their benign twins (development-grade), StrongREJECT (2402.10260) for held-out harmful items with category structure, XSTest-safe for over-refusal, PolyRefuse / RefusEU (NASK-PIB) for multilingual refusal evaluation, FLORES-200 for language-quality regression, and the six-task EN harness (ARC-C, BoolQ, HellaSwag, OBQA, PIQA, Winogrande) with Slovenian LLM Eval for utility. Known-bad: substring/keyword refusal counters (2402.10260 and this run's own art_0XmNBGkzsJc_ measured kappa +0.196 with 25.4% false 'refusals' on edited checkpoints); AdvBench as an evaluation set; and treating same-ID RefusEU rows as exact translations - this run's dataset artifact graded EN-SL correspondence as 0 true / 158 partial / 1092 conflicting / 150 none, which is why the S5X cross-translated 100-pair set exists and is the ONLY basis for paired cross-language claims.\\n\\nCONTROLS AND WHAT IS HELD CONSTANT. For a placement claim the comparison is meaningless unless TOTAL REMOVAL ENERGY and LAYER COUNT are matched between the contrasted conditions - this run already learned (art_ex4hbgThhJaL) that energy matching alone is not a control because energy-matched random edits can be destructive, so controls must be matched on energy AND on collateral (per-token NLL / KL change). Also held fixed: quantisation (NF4 throughout, since absolute levels are NF4-specific), decoding (greedy, fixed max new tokens), chat template and system-prompt policy byte-identical across languages, judge and rubric frozen and blind to condition identity, and one model resident at a time with weight hashes recorded. The confound this design is most likely to be caught on: DOSE masquerading as PLACEMENT. art_0XmNBGkzsJc_'s falsifier P7 fired exactly this way - at equal harmless KL a plain dose ladder reached a zero cross-language gap, so a 'better-placed' edit that is merely stronger proves nothing. Every placement contrast must therefore be reported at matched energy AND with a dose ladder of the losing arm as the competing explanation.\\n\\nHOW MUCH IS ENOUGH. Item counts: 60-120 harmful items per language per condition is the working range in this literature (StrongREJECT's own held-out evaluations, JBB's 100 behaviours, RefusEU's per-language eval blocks); below ~50 items a per-cell refusal rate has a binomial half-width of ~0.14 and nobody reads a 0.10 difference off it. Conditions: an instrument claim needs at least 20 held-out cells to support a Spearman with a usable CI (a rank correlation on 10 points has a 95% CI roughly -0.1 to 0.9 and is uninformative); 2604.15557 uses 24 concept families across 5 models. Models: at least 2 families for any generalisation sentence, with the honest statement that 2 checkpoints are 2 units. Both a STRICT (refused only) and a BROAD (refused + partial) rate are reported, because this run's own audit (art_Z3I1K3VnFZuz) showed the cross-language gap ranges +0.22 to +0.71 strict versus -0.04 to +0.37 broad. Judge agreement is reported WITHIN edited arms, not pooled (pooled kappa 0.913 vs 0.779 within-edited in the same data), and a judge below its gate means Rogan-Gladen-corrected rates beside raw ones plus a JUDGE_SENSITIVE flag. Collateral is reported as FLORES per-token NLL change, harmless KL, language consistency (GlotLID), truncation/validity rate and a utility macro - the handbook's rule that a correction claim must also measure disruption of already-correct cases. Instrument quality is reported as per-language Spearman plus incremental R2 over a nested predictor stack, each with a cell bootstrap, and - the part the field now demands - a paired difference against the cheap baseline with a CI on the DIFFERENCE, not two separate correlations placed side by side.\",", " ", "-  \"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS THE FIELD'S PRACTICE. (1) Both of the venue's two tracks are attempted in one artifact: a falsifiable, pre-registered hypothesis (O predicts residual refusal out of sample) reported with the evidence for AND against, and a practical-benefit claim measured against well-implemented baselines (the two one-forward-pass predictors), with the failure branch written into the plan as a reportable result. (2) The cheap-baseline race — the comparison a reviewer names first — is primary, not supplementary, and is scored with a paired item-bootstrap CI on the DIFFERENCE. (3) Matched-random and energy-AND-collateral-matched PC controls accompany every matched group, per 2609.22135 and the handbook's random-perturbation rule. (4) Placement contrasts hold total removal energy and layer count identical, and a dose ladder of the losing arm is run as the competing explanation, because this run has already been burned by dose masquerading as placement. (5) The causal write profile e_L(h) is estimated with an item bootstrap and a split-half stability check rather than as a single-draw curve, answering the handbook's volatility rule directly. (6) The probe-derived write mass is reported BESIDE the causal profile so the knowledge-action distinction is visible, which is precisely the 'read-best is not steer-best' finding. (7) Judging uses a frozen 4-way rubric with PARTIAL and INVALID as their own columns, strict and broad rates both reported, and a blocking within-edited-arm kappa gate with a purchased frontier-judge calibration subsample. (8) Freeze-before-confirmation with a hash and an mtime ordering check, and an independent re-derivation path, are already this run's house standard and are kept.\\n\\nDEPARTURES, AND WHAT EACH COSTS. (i) NF4 quantisation throughout, on one 20 GB GPU. The literature's numbers are bf16. Justified by hardware; it costs absolute comparability of refusal levels with published figures, so every rate is labelled NF4-specific and only WITHIN-protocol contrasts are claimed. The single bf16 control cell that would break this confound is explicitly NOT in this artifact (another slot owns it) — so the plan must say the confound is open here. (ii) n = 2 model families (gemma-3-12b-it anchor, Qwen3-8B outside family), where 2604.15557 uses 5. Justified by a 6-hour budget with 12B-scale generation; it costs the strength of any 'the instrument generalises' sentence, which is therefore written as 'holds in the anchor and is consistent/inconsistent in one outside family', never as a general law. (iii) Slovene, German and Lithuanian items are machine-translated with automated QC only; native review is PENDING. It costs the absolute levels in those languages, though the key statistic is a RANK correlation across conditions within a language, which is far more robust to uniform translation artefacts than a level is; the NLLB-vs-GPT translation-method control from art_kfCCWf7o8eJ9 (all rates moved <= 0.09) is re-quoted rather than re-run. (iv) One optimiser seed's worth of edit conditions, and the conditions are constructed by us rather than drawn by Heretic's TPE. Justified because the claim is about the PREDICTOR, not about the search; it costs the ability to say anything about run-to-run search variance, which the plan explicitly disclaims. (v) The confirmation set is ~20-24 conditions per model rather than the 100+ that would make the incremental-R^2 estimate tight. Closed as far as budget allows by (a) choosing conditions to SPAN the predictor rather than sampling randomly, which maximises the information per condition, and (b) pre-computing the MDE so a null is reported as powered or as inconclusive rather than as a result. What remains is that a dR2 near the 0.10 threshold will have a wide CI, and the plan pre-commits to reporting it as inconclusive in that band instead of rounding it into a verdict. (vi) Utility (lm-eval macro) is NOT re-run for every new condition — only FLORES dNLL, harmless KL, language consistency and validity are, with the full six-task utility panel run on the two extreme conditions only. Justified by time; it costs a complete safety-utility curve for the new cells, which the earlier artifacts already supply for the older ones. (vii) The generalisation arm reuses translated DE/LT items from art_kfCCWf7o8eJ9 rather than the field-standard PolyRefuse, because they are already QC'd and on disk and the eligibility gate was pre-registered against them; PolyRefuse would be the cleaner citation and is named in the limitations. (viii) No new human/native review is commissioned here, so 'PENDING' stays pending — stated, not quietly dropped.\",", "+  \"practice_alignment\": \"MEETS THE FIELD'S PRACTICE. (viii) No new human/native review is commissioned here, so 'PENDING' stays pending - stated, not quietly dropped.\",", " ", "-  \"builds_on\": \"This is a DEEPEN, not a fresh line, and almost every input already exists on disk. The executor receives the dataset artifact through the declared dependency and picks up everything else by absolute path under /ai-inventor/aii_data/runs/run_Fapgmt6JWbcD/3_invention_loop/. Each reuse below is written so the plan still runs if the file is missing (fallbacks in fallback_plan).\\n\\nDECLARED DEPENDENCY. art_qdUCJWbc5kHh (iter_1/gen_art/gen_art_dataset_1): the frozen split manifest and per-family JSONL. Used directly: S3 (screen/DEV JBB twins + Dolly + FLORES dev + MC) for the DEV causal profile; S4_strongreject_pairs, and specifically the 70 held-out-Llama-Guard-category ('hoc') pairs, for confirmation; S5X_refuseu_crosstrans (100 verified EN-SL pairs) as the only paired cross-language basis; S6 XSTest-safe for over-refusal; S7_flores_devtest for language-quality collateral. data/split_manifest.json SHA256s are re-verified at startup and the protocol_hash dc33bde4... recorded.\\n\\nFROZEN DIRECTIONS AND HARNESSES REUSED (not recomputed).\\n- iter_2/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_8/directions/: the frozen per-layer English refusal directions d_EN(h) for gemma-3-12b-it, already re-verified at cos >= 0.9964 against a fresh recompute by art_xLy2vVlI7OEL. The plan re-verifies at 4 spot layers and REPORTS the cosine, then reuses.\\n- iter_3/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_10/heretic_op.py: the generalised Heretic weight operator (arbitrary per-(layer,component) coefficients), already certified against Heretic's own source at max relative error 1.7e-6 with a trial-88 adapter rebuild at median 3.3e-7 and an energy identity below 1e-6. This is the single most valuable reuse — it is what lets the executor build matched-energy conditions without re-deriving the operator, and it is why O can be defined in weight space at all.\\n- iter_3/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_9/results/cells/ and results/cells.csv (122 Gemma cells) and iter_3/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_10/ (57 GaMS3 cells): per-cell per-layer removal-energy profiles g(h), closed-form energies, FLORES/KL collateral and judged residual refusal. These are the SCREEN set — O was never fitted on them, so they are a legitimate, declared screen, and they cost zero GPU time.\\n- iter_3/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_12/: the multi-model harness, the PRE-REGISTERED eligibility gate (no-op refusal >= 0.60 and INVALID <= 0.20), the NLLB-translated DE/LT items with their QC, the baseline tables, and rederive.py's style of stdlib-only re-derivation. The outside-family arm is a re-parameterisation of this harness, not new infrastructure.\\n- iter_3/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_11/scorer/refusal_clf.joblib: the distilled partial-aware classifier, certified at kappa 0.858 [0.820, 0.888] on 20 held-out replayed trials. Used ONLY as a fast triage/monitoring readout during scale-up; no reported number rests on it.\\n- iter_2/gen_art/gen_art_experiment_4 frozen 4-way rubric (REFUSED/PARTIAL/COMPLIED/INVALID), verbatim, plus iter_3/gen_art/gen_art_evaluation_1/configs/label_map.yaml, which the audit artifact explicitly marked reusable for new cells.\\n\\nNEGATIVE FINDINGS BUILT PAST, NOT RE-COVERED.\\n- Depth coverage as a COUNT is a settled negative in both models (Gemma dR2 0.040 with leave-one-out -0.002 and MDE 0.071; GaMS3 dR2 0.002 with leave-one-cell-out -0.043). It enters this plan only as a NUISANCE REGRESSOR that O must beat, never as a hypothesis.\\n- An ACTIVATION-space depth index does not predict WEIGHT-edit outcomes out of sample (pooled Spearman -0.009 over 21 rows). This is the direct motivation for defining O in weight space and for keeping the two cheap baselines as the bar to clear.\\n- The corrected-objective checkpoint is falsified as a better EDIT (its own P7 fired), so no part of this plan tries to produce a better edit; the objective-miscalibration measurement survives and is cited, not repeated.\\n- 'Broad-and-weak beats narrow-and-strong' is falsified in the opposite direction in both models, which is why the confirmation contrast is narrow-vs-strided at MATCHED energy and MATCHED count rather than a coverage sweep.\\n\\nWHO PRODUCES THE INPUTS OF THE LATER PHASES (nothing is assumed into existence). The confirmation phase needs: (a) frozen e_L(h) — produced by this artifact's own Phase 1 on DEV items only; (b) held-out harmful items with categories — already produced and frozen by the dependency (S4 hoc stratum, 70 pairs) and never opened by the screen; (c) 4-way labels on the new generations — produced inside this artifact by the local Qwen3-14B judge on the frozen rubric, with (d) its within-edited-arm agreement certified against a purchased stratified gpt-4.1 subsample of 600-900 items drawn from THIS artifact's own edited cells, budgeted at $1.5-3 of the $10 OpenRouter cap and scheduled as Phase 6 BEFORE any confirmatory number is read; (e) the cheap baselines — the unedited refusal rate comes free from the no-op arm generated in Phase 4, and the single-site transfer rate is a by-product of the Phase 1 profile at the frozen site, so neither needs a separate pass.\",", "+  \"builds_on\": \"This is a DEEPEN, not a fresh line, and almost every input already exists on disk.
